```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences a.

The implementation combines:
  * L-BFGS on a softmax parameterization with a log-sum-exp annealed objective,
  * active-set style gradient polishing in log-space,
  * a KKT-weight refinement hill-climber,
  * a max-entropy cross-correlation direction (a heuristic analogue of the
    linear-programming direction from the previous state of the art),
  * multi-length search and iterated local search.

All sequences are normalized to sum 1 internally.  The returned sequence is
non-negative and has sum 1, which is safe for evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# basic FFT / convolution utilities
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _next_pow2_ge(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = len(b)
    out = n + m - 1
    N = _next_pow2_ge(out)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)
    return c[:out]


def _self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    out = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2_ge(out)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    return c[:out]


def _corr_hp(p, w):
    """
    h_j = sum_i p_i w_{i+j}, j=0..n-1.
    Uses an FFT length large enough to avoid aliasing in the needed middle lags.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    N = _next_pow2_ge(2 * n)
    wr = w[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * W, N)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# normalization / score
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(a))
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _self_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _score1(c, n):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# softmax and smooth objective / gradient
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-50.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / len(z)
    return e / s


def _to_z(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-50.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _weights_from_c(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    num = len(c)
    if num == 0 or not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        if num:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    if beta <= 0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        beta = 1e-300
    sh = (c - m) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s
    return w


def _smooth_obj(z, beta, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _self_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        return 0.0
    sh = (c - m) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 2.0 * n * m
    return float(2.0 * n * (m + beta * np.log(s)))


def _smooth_grad(z, beta, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _self_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(len(a), dtype=np.float64)
    w = _weights_from_c(c, beta)
    h = _corr_hp(a, w)
    W = float(np.dot(w, c))
    grad = 4.0 * n * a * (h - W)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return grad


class _SmoothProblem:
    def __init__(self, beta, n):
        self.beta = beta
        self.n = n

    def f(self, z):
        return _smooth_obj(z, self.beta, self.n)

    def g(self, z):
        return _smooth_grad(z, self.beta, self.n)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# profile generation / resampling
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _make_profile(n, kind="uniform", seed=12345):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "triangle":
        a = 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * t - 1.0)
    elif kind == "hann":
        a = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    elif kind == "gaussian":
        a = np.exp(-4.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 2)
    elif kind == "raised_cos2":
        a = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)) ** 2
    elif kind == "super_gauss":
        a = np.exp(-8.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 6)
    elif kind == "sine":
        a = np.sin(np.pi * t)
    else:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        a = rng.random(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=1e-12, posinf=1e-12, neginf=1e-12)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def _resample(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    old_n = a.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, a[0] / new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(b)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS annealing
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _chunk_maxit(n, deadline, min_rem):
    rem = max(1.0, deadline - min_rem - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 5.0:
        return 20
    if n <= 512:
        return 6000
    if n <= 1024:
        return 3500
    if n <= 2048:
        return 1800
    if n <= 4096:
        return 1000
    if n <= 8192:
        return 500
    if n <= 16384:
        return 240
    if n <= 32768:
        return 120
    if n <= 65536:
        return 60
    return 30


def _lbfgs_anneal(a, deadline, min_remaining=20.0):
    n = len(a)
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    z = _to_z(a)
    best_z = z.copy()

    factors = (
        0.10, 0.07, 0.05, 0.035, 0.025, 0.018, 0.013, 0.010,
        0.0075, 0.0055, 0.004, 0.003, 0.0022, 0.0016, 0.0012,
        0.0009, 0.00065, 0.0005, 0.00037, 0.00028, 0.00021,
        0.00016, 0.00012, 0.00009, 0.00007, 0.00005, 0.00004,
        0.00003, 0.000022, 0.000016, 0.000012, 0.000009,
        0.0000065, 0.000005, 0.0000038, 0.0000028, 0.0000021,
        0.0000016, 0.0000012, 0.0000009, 0.0000007
    )

    stagnant = 0
    max_betas = 42

    for fac in factors[:max_betas]:
        if time.monotonic() + min_remaining >= deadline:
            break

        c = _self_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            break

        beta = max(M * fac, M * 1e-12)
        if beta <= 0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
            continue

        prob = _SmoothProblem(beta, n)
        current_fun = None
        worsened_in_a_row = 0

        while time.monotonic() + min_remaining < deadline:
            maxit = _chunk_maxit(n, deadline, min_remaining)
            maxcor = 30 if n > 32768 else 50
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    prob.f, z, jac=prob.g, method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxit,
                        "ftol": 1e-16,
                        "gtol": 1e-13,
                        "maxcor": maxcor,
                        "maxls": 30
                    }
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x"):
                break

            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                break

            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
            a_new = _softmax(x)
            sc_new = _score(a_new)

            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_z = x.copy()

            new_fun = None
            if hasattr(res, "fun") and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                new_fun = float(res.fun)

            if new_fun is None:
                if res.status != 1:
                    break
                continue

            if current_fun is None:
                current_fun = new_fun

            if new_fun < current_fun - 1e-20:
                a = a_new.copy()
                z = x.copy()
                current_fun = new_fun
                worsened_in_a_row = 0
            else:
                worsened_in_a_row += 1
                if worsened_in_a_row >= 3:
                    break

            if res.status != 1:
                break

        if best_sc < best_sc if False else True:  # placeholder for linter
            pass

        # Use a small variable to track improvement per beta.
        # (The previous code already updates best_sc globally.)

        # If the current point is much worse than the best, revert to best.
        sc_cur = _score(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_sc + 1e-5:
            a = best_a.copy()
            z = _to_z(a)

        # Break if we have made no true improvement for many fine betas.
        # This is approximate: we track a local counter externally by comparing
        # best_sc before/after each beta.
        # To avoid adding more temporaries here, use a simple heuristic based on
        # factor: by very small beta with no time left, stop.
        if fac < 1e-5 and time.monotonic() + 30.0 >= deadline:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# active-set / smoothed-max polishing
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _active_polish(a, deadline, min_remaining=5.0,
                   beta_factor_start=1e-3,
                   beta_factor_end=1e-7,
                   step0=0.10,
                   rng=None):
    n = len(a)
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    z = _to_z(a)
    best_z = z.copy()

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    step = step0
    beta_factor = beta_factor_start
    momentum = 0.9
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.125, 0.05)

    while time.monotonic() + min_remaining < deadline:
        it += 1
        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _self_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            best_z = z.copy()

        denom = max(M * beta_factor, 1e-300)
        w = _weights_from_c(c, denom)
        h = _corr_hp(a, w)
        W = float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad = 4.0 * n * a * (h - W)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-18:
            break

        direction = -grad / gmax
        direction -= float(np.mean(direction))

        vel = momentum * vel + direction
        vnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(vel)))
        if not np.isfinite(vnorm) or vnorm < 1e-18:
            vel = direction.copy()
            vnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(vel)))
            if vnorm < 1e-18:
                break
        vdir = vel / vnorm

        best_local = sc
        best_local_z = z
        chosen_alpha = None

        for mult in multipliers:
            alpha = step * mult
            zt = z + alpha * vdir
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(zt)):
                continue
            at = _softmax(zt)
            ct = _self_conv(at)
            sc_t = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(ct))
            if np.isfinite(sc_t) and sc_t < best_local - 1e-15:
                best_local = sc_t
                best_local_z = zt.copy()
                chosen_alpha = alpha

        if chosen_alpha is not None:
            z = best_local_z
            if sc_local_is_better := (best_local < best_sc - 1e-15):
                a = _softmax(z)
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_sc = best_local
                best_z = z.copy()
            else:
                a = _softmax(z)
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_sc = best_local
                best_z = z.copy()
            stall = 0

            if chosen_alpha > step * 1.2:
                step = min(0.5, step * 1.15)
            elif chosen_alpha < step * 0.8:
                step = max(1e-5, step * 0.70)
            beta_factor = max(beta_factor_end, beta_factor * 0.99)
        else:
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            stall += 1
            step = max(1e-5, step * 0.70)
            beta_factor = max(beta_factor_end, beta_factor * 0.70)
            if stall > 80:
                z = best_z.copy()
                z = z + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
                step = step0
                beta_factor = max(beta_factor_start, 5e-4)
                vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                stall = 0

        if it % 100 == 0:
            a2 = _normalize(a + a[::-1])
            sc2 = _score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < float(np.max(_self_conv(a))) * 2.0 * n - 1e-12:
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_sc = sc2
                    z = _to_z(a2)
                    best_z = z.copy()
                    a = a2.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# KKT-weight hill climber
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _kkt_hill(a, deadline, min_remaining=10.0, rng=None):
    n = len(a)
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    z = _to_z(a)
    best_z = z.copy()

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)

    step = 0.10
    step_w = 0.6
    beta_wfac = 1e-3
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.125)

    while time.monotonic() + min_remaining < deadline:
        it += 1
        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _self_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            best_z = z.copy()

        denom = max(M * beta_wfac, 1e-300)
        w = _weights_from_c(c, denom)

        log_w = np.log(np.maximum(w, 1e-300))
        log_w = np.clip(log_w - float(np.max(log_w)), -50.0, 0.0)

        # Refine weights so that H_w a is flatter.
        weight_iters = 32
        for _ in range(weight_iters):
            if time.monotonic() + min_remaining >= deadline:
                break
            h1 = _corr_hp(a, w)
            target = float(np.dot(a, h1))
            r = h1 - target
            gw = _conv(a, r)
            upd = -gw
            upd -= float(np.mean(upd))
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(upd)))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-300:
                break
            upd = upd / scale
            log_w = log_w + step_w * upd
            log_w = np.clip(log_w - float(np.max(log_w)), -50.0, 0.0)
            ww = np.exp(log_w)
            s = float(np.sum(ww))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                break
            w = ww / s

        h = _corr_hp(a, w)
        target = float(np.dot(a, h))
        grad = 4.0 * n * a * (h - target)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-18:
            break

        direction = -grad / gmax
        direction -= float(np.mean(direction))

        best_local = sc
        best_local_z = z
        chosen_alpha = None

        for mult in multipliers:
            alpha = step * mult
            zt = z + alpha * direction
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(zt)):
                continue
            at = _softmax(zt)
            ct = _self_conv(at)
            sc_t = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(ct))
            if np.isfinite(sc_t) and sc_t < best_local - 1e-15:
                best_local = sc_t
                best_local_z = zt.copy()
                chosen_alpha = alpha

        if chosen_alpha is not None:
            z = best_local_z
            a = _softmax(z)
            if best_local < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_sc = best_local
                best_z = z.copy()
            stall = 0

            if chosen_alpha > step * 1.2:
                step = min(0.5, step * 1.15)
            elif chosen_alpha < step * 0.8:
                step = max(1e-5, step * 0.70)
            beta_wfac = max(1e-8, beta_wfac * 0.96)
        else:
            stall += 1
            step = max(1e-5, step * 0.70)
            beta_wfac = min(1e-2, beta_wfac * 1.5)
            if stall > 70:
                z = best_z.copy()
                z = z + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
                step = 0.10
                beta_wfac = 1e-3
                stall = 0

        if it % 100 == 0:
            a2 = _normalize(a + a[::-1])
            sc2 = _score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_a = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                z = _to_z(a2)
                best_z = z.copy()
                a = a2.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# max-entropy cross-correlation direction
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _entropy_direction(a, max_iters=300, time_budget=5.0, eta=0.5):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    log_y = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = float(eta)

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget:
            break

        log_y = np.clip(log_y - float(np.max(log_y)), -50.0, 0.0)
        y = np.exp(log_y)
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            y = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(L)
        y = y / s * L

        h = _corr_hp(a, y)
        h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
        g = 1.0 / h
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=1.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=1.0)

        v = _conv(a, g)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        vmax = float(np.max(v))
        if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 0:
            break

        r = v / vmax
        mean_r = float(np.mean(r))
        rc = r - mean_r

        # Increase y where the cross-correlation is large, decrease mildly
        # where it is small.
        update = np.empty_like(rc)
        pos = rc >= 0.0
        update[pos] = rc[pos]
        update[~pos] = 0.3 * rc[~pos]
        update = np.clip(update, -1.0, 1.0)

        log_y = log_y + eta * update
        log_y = np.nan_to_num(log_y, nan=-50.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)

    log_y = np.clip(log_y - float(np.max(log_y)), -50.0, 0.0)
    y = np.exp(log_y)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    y = y / s * L
    h = _corr_hp(a, y)
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
    g = 1.0 / h
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return _normalize(g)


def _mix_improve(a, g):
    a = _normalize(a)
    g = _normalize(g)
    sc = _score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    sim = float(np.dot(a, g))
    if not np.isfinite(sim) or sim > 1.0 - 1e-7:
        return best, best_sc

    alphas = (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5)
    for alpha in alphas:
        m = a + alpha * (g - a)
        mn = float(np.min(m))
        if mn < -1e-8:
            m = np.maximum(m, 0.0)
        m = _normalize(m)
        scm = _score(m)
        if np.isfinite(scm) and scm < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = m.copy()
            best_sc = scm

    return best, best_sc


def _entropy_hill(a, deadline, min_remaining=10.0, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    stall = 0

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(31415)

    while time.monotonic() + min_remaining < deadline:
        inner_time = min(6.0, deadline - min_remaining - time.monotonic())
        if inner_time < 1.5:
            break

        g = _entropy_direction(best, max_iters=260, time_budget=max(0.1, inner_time * 0.75), eta=0.5)
        a2, sc2 = _mix_improve(best, g)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 4:
                bp = _perturb(best, 2, rng, 0.05)
                g2 = _entropy_direction(bp, max_iters=180, time_budget=max(0.1, inner_time * 0.45), eta=0.5)
                b2, scb2 = _mix_improve(bp, g2)
                if np.isfinite(scb2) and scb2 < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best = b2.copy()
                    best_sc = scb2
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    sym = _normalize(best + best[::-1])
                    scs = _score(sym)
                    if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best = sym.copy()
                        best_sc = scs
                        stall = 0
                    else:
                        break

    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# perturbations and ILS
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _perturb(a, kind, rng, strength=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    z = _to_z(a)

    if kind == 0:
        s = 0.05 if strength is None else float(strength)
        z = z + s * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 1:
        s = 0.15 if strength is None else float(strength)
        z = z + s * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 2:
        block = max(20, n // 12)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - block))
        z = z.copy()
        z[start:start + block] = z[start:start + block] + (0.10 if strength is None else float(strength)) * rng.standard_normal(block)
    elif kind == 3:
        seg = max(20, n // 25)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - seg))
        z = z.copy()
        z[start:start + seg] = z[start:start + seg][::-1]
        z = z + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 4:
        shift = int(rng.integers(-max(1, n // 120), max(1, n // 120) + 1))
        a2 = np.roll(a, shift)
        return _normalize(a2)
    else:
        m = 5
        amps = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.08, m)
        freqs = rng.integers(1, 6, m)
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, m)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(m):
            pert = pert + amps[i] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freqs[i]) * x + phases[i])
        s = 0.20 if strength is None else float(strength)
        z = z + s * pert / max(1e-12, float(np.max(np.abs(pert))))

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return _softmax(z)


def _quick_ils(a, deadline, rng):
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)

    # quick active polish
    if time.monotonic() + 3.0 < deadline:
        d1 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(3.0, 0.65 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        a2, sc2 = _active_polish(a, d1, min_remaining=2.0,
                                 beta_factor_start=1e-3,
                                 beta_factor_end=1e-6,
                                 step0=0.08,
                                 rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            a = best.copy()

    # optional fast entropy step
    if time.monotonic() + 2.5 < deadline:
        d2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(2.0, 0.30 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        a3, sc3 = _entropy_hill(a, d2, min_remaining=1.5, rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a3.copy()
            best_sc = sc3

    return best, best_sc


def _ils(a, deadline, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(54321)

    current = _normalize(a.copy())
    current_sc = _score(current)
    best = current.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    attempt = 0
    while time.monotonic() + 12.0 < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break

        qb = max(4.0, min(14.0, 0.14 * rem))
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
        strength = 0.03 + 0.02 * (attempt % 5)

        a_p = _perturb(current, kind, rng, strength)
        d = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + qb)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _quick_ils(a_p, d, rng)

        if np.isfinite(sc_opt):
            if sc_opt < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = a_opt.copy()
                best_sc = sc_opt
                current = a_opt.copy()
                current_sc = sc_opt
            elif sc_opt < current_sc - 1e-7:
                current = a_opt.copy()
                current_sc = sc_opt

        attempt += 1
        if attempt % 25 == 0:
            print(f"    ILS attempt {attempt}, local best {best_sc:.10f}, remaining {max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()):.1f}s")

    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# full local pipeline
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _local_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a.copy())
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc) or now + 25.0 >= deadline:
        return best, best_sc

    total = deadline - now

    # Cheap global symmetrization.
    sym = _normalize(a + a[::-1])
    scs = _score(sym)
    if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best = sym.copy()
        best_sc = scs
        a = best.copy()

    # L-BFGS.
    if total > 60.0:
        d = min(deadline, now + 0.42 * total)
        a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_anneal(a, d, min_remaining=15.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            a = best.copy()

    # KKT weights.
    if time.monotonic() + 25.0 < deadline:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        d = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, 0.28 * remaining))
        a2, sc2 = _kkt_hill(a, d, min_remaining=10.0, rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            a = best.copy()

    # Active polish.
    if time.monotonic() + 12.0 < deadline:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        d = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(10.0, 0.22 * remaining))
        a2, sc2 = _active_polish(a, d, min_remaining=5.0,
                                 beta_factor_start=1e-3,
                                 beta_factor_end=1e-7,
                                 step0=0.09,
                                 rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            a = best.copy()

    # Entropy direction.
    if time.monotonic() + 12.0 < deadline:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        d = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(10.0, 0.20 * remaining))
        a2, sc2 = _entropy_hill(a, d, min_remaining=5.0, rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            a = best.copy()

    # ILS with remaining time.
    if time.monotonic() + 35.0 < deadline:
        a2, sc2 = _ils(a, deadline - 10.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2

    # Final symmetrization.
    sym = _normalize(best + best[::-1])
    scs = _score(sym)
    if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best = sym.copy()
        best_sc = scs

    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# main entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng((int(seed) % 1_000_000_007) + 7919)

    print(f"budget={budget:.1f}s, deadline reserve={deadline-t0:.1f}s")

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0:
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(np.sum(arr))
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.01:
                parent = arr / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = _make_profile(24576, "uniform")
        print("No parent sequence found; using uniform n=24576.")

    best_seq = parent.copy()
    best_score = _score(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = _make_profile(24576, "uniform")
        best_score = _score(best_seq)

    print(f"Initial parent n={len(best_seq)}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Quick evaluation of candidate lengths.
    if budget > 1500.0:
        lengths = [24576, 28000, 30000, 31000, 32000, 32768, 40000, 45000, 49152, 55000, 65536]
    elif budget > 900.0:
        lengths = [24576, 30000, 32000, 32768, 49152]
    else:
        lengths = [24576, 32768]

    quick = []
    quick_dict = {}
    for nn in lengths:
        if nn < 256 or nn > 98304:
            continue
        a0 = _resample(parent, nn)
        sc0 = _score(a0)
        if np.isfinite(sc0):
            quick.append((sc0, int(nn), a0))
            if int(nn) not in quick_dict:
                quick_dict[int(nn)] = (sc0, a0)

    quick.sort()

    selected_ns = []
    selected_init = {}

    if quick:
        sc_b, nn_b, a_b = quick[0]
        selected_ns.append(int(nn_b))
        selected_init[int(nn_b)] = a_b

    max_selected = 4 if budget > 1600.0 else 3
    target_order = [32768]
    if budget > 1200.0:
        target_order.append(49152)
    if budget > 1700.0:
        target_order.append(65536)

    for tgt in target_order:
        if len(selected_ns) >= max_selected:
            break
        if tgt in quick_dict and tgt not in selected_ns:
            selected_ns.append(tgt)
            selected_init[tgt] = quick_dict[tgt][1]

    if len(selected_ns) < 2 and budget > 700.0:
        for tgt in [32768, 49152]:
            if len(selected_ns) >= max_selected:
                break
            if tgt not in selected_ns:
                selected_ns.append(tgt)
                if tgt in quick_dict:
                    selected_init[tgt] = quick_dict[tgt][1]
                else:
                    selected_init[tgt] = _resample(parent, tgt)

    if not selected_ns:
        nn = 32768 if budget > 900 else 24576
        selected_ns = [nn]
        selected_init[nn] = _resample(parent, nn)

    print("Selected lengths:", selected_ns)

    # Small-resolution global search to provide a diverse initialization.
    small_init = None
    if budget > 1000.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 900.0:
        small_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + 90.0, deadline - 900.0)
        n_small = 512
        small_best = None
        small_best_sc = np.inf
        kinds = ("uniform", "hann", "gaussian", "raised_cos2", "sine", "random")
        small_budget = min(16.0, (small_deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, len(kinds)) * 1.8)
        for idx, kind in enumerate(kinds):
            if small_deadline - time.monotonic() < small_budget + 3.0:
                break
            a0 = _make_profile(n_small, kind, seed=int(seed) + 1000 + idx * 17)
            d = min(small_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(8.0, small_budget))
            a1, sc1 = _local_optimize(a0, d, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < small_best_sc:
                small_best_sc = sc1
                small_best = a1.copy()
        if small_best is not None:
            small_init = small_best
            print(f"Small-search best n={len(small_init)}, score={small_best_sc:.10f}")

    # Build initializations per selected length.
    inits_by_n = {}
    for nn in selected_ns:
        inits = []
        if nn in selected_init:
            inits.append(selected_init[nn])
        else:
            inits.append(_resample(parent, nn))
        if small_init is not None:
            inits.append(_resample(small_init, nn))
        inits_by_n[nn] = inits

    # Main multi-length search.
    final_reserve = min(320.0, max(120.0, 0.15 * budget))
    search_deadline = max(deadline - final_reserve, deadline - 0.5)
    if search_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        search_deadline = deadline

    shares = (0.48, 0.34, 0.24, 0.18)
    tried_ns = set(selected_ns)

    for idx, nn in enumerate(selected_ns):
        rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 90.0:
            break
        share = shares[idx] if idx < len(shares) else 0.18
        total_phase = max(90.0, share * rem)
        start = time.monotonic()
        phase_deadline = min(search_deadline, start + total_phase)

        inits = inits_by_n.get(nn, [_resample(parent, nn)])
        if len(inits) == 0:
            inits = [_resample(parent, nn)]
        elif len(inits) == 1:
            sub_deadlines = [phase_deadline]
        else:
            sub_deadlines = [start + 0.68 * total_phase, phase_deadline]

        if len(inits) > len(sub_deadlines):
            sub_deadlines = [phase_deadline] * len(inits)

        for j, a0 in enumerate(inits[:len(sub_deadlines)]):
            sub_d = sub_deadlines[j]
            if time.monotonic() + 50.0 >= sub_d:
                break
            print(f"  [n={nn}] init {j+1}/{len(inits[:len(sub_deadlines)])}, phase remaining {sub_d-time.monotonic():.1f}s")
            a2, sc2 = _local_optimize(a0, sub_d, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_seq = a2.copy()
                best_score = sc2
                print(f"    NEW BEST n={len(best_seq)}, score={best_score:.10f}")

        # If this length is very good and time remains, try a nearby untried length.
        if time.monotonic() + 120.0 < search_deadline and len(best_seq) == nn:
            extra = int(round(nn * 1.15))
            if extra not in tried_ns and 256 <= extra <= 98304:
                tries = [extra]
                for q in tries:
                    if time.monotonic() + 90.0 >= search_deadline:
                        break
                    if q in tried_ns:
                        continue
                    tried_ns.add(q)
                    a0 = _resample(best_seq, q)
                    sc0 = _score(a0)
                    if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score + 0.02:
                        d = min(search_deadline, time.monotonic() + 130.0)
                        a2, sc2 = _local_optimize(a0, d, rng)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                            best_seq = a2.copy()
                            best_score = sc2
                            print(f"    EXTRA NEW BEST n={len(best_seq)}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Final polish on the global best sequence.
    final_deadline = deadline - 5.0
    if time.monotonic() + 20.0 < final_deadline:
        print(f"Final polish on n={len(best_seq)} with {final_deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}s")
        a_final, sc_final = _local_optimize(best_seq, final_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_score:
            best_seq = a_final.copy()
            best_score = sc_final

    # Safety validation.
    out = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        out = _make_profile(256, "uniform")

    final_score = _score(out)
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL returned n={len(out)}, score={final_score:.10f}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
